Algorithm: sort v descending; find rho such that v_i - τ >0. For sum=1, τ = (sum v_pos -1)/count. Standard:
```
u=np.sort(v)[::-1]
cssv=np.cumsum(u)
rho=np.nonzero(u*(np.arange(1,n+1)-1) > cssv-1)[0][-1]
theta=(cssv[rho]-1)/(rho+1)
w=np.maximum(v-theta,0)
return w
```
Works. If v sum already 1 and nonnegative, returns same.

Now, can we make a specific strong algorithm that likely reaches target? Let's refine local optimizer with active set and line search. Pseudocode:

```
def local_refine(a, deadline, rng, verbose=True):
    a = normalize(a)
    n = a.size
    b_size = next_pow2(2*n-1)
    g_size = next_pow2(3*n-2) # for gradient? We can reuse b_size if weights length 2n-1 convolved with a length n -> length 3n-2. Need g_size.
    b = conv_fft(a,b_size)
    M, score
    best_a = a.copy(); best_score=score
    beta_target=30
    eta=0.3
    smooth= lse(b, beta_target/M)
    fail=0; no_improve=0
    topk=4
    while time<deadline:
       b = conv_fft(a,b_size); M=max; score
       if score<best: update; no_improve=0
       else no_improve++
       beta = min(1e6, max(1.0, beta_target / max(M,1e-12)))
       # active weights
       if no_improve > 200 or topk>64: topk = min(2*n-1, topk*2) else topk=4?
       # p: sort? Sorting every iter O(n log n) okay for n<=2048 but many iterations. Could use threshold: idx = np.flatnonzero(b > 0.9*M) plus maybe top 16 via argpartition.
       idx = np.argpartition(b, -K)[-K:]
       vals = b[idx]
       w = np.exp(beta*(vals - M)); p zeros; p[idx]=w/sum
       grad = weighted_grad(a,p,g_size,n)
       grad_c = grad - np.mean(grad)
       gnorm = max(abs(grad_c))
       if gnorm==0: perturb
       # Bregman update:
       d = grad_c / (np.std(a)+gn? maybe use gnorm)
       # Try several lr: eta, eta/2, eta/4, -? (ascent maybe)
       best_step = None; best_val = score (or smooth?)
       for m in [eta, eta*1.5, eta*0.5, eta*0.25]:
           a2 = a * np.exp(-m*d)
           a2 = normalize(a2)
           b2=conv; s2; smooth2?
           if s2 < best_step: step
       if found improves score: accept eta=...; fail=0; beta_target slight inc
       else:
           # Euclidean with line search
           d2 = grad_c / gnorm
           for m in [eta*mean, eta*0.5*mean,...]:
              a2=project(a - m*d2)
              evaluate
           if accept, else fail++
       if fail>50: perturb best; a=perturb; fail=0; beta_target=max(8,beta_target*0.5)
       if no_improve>500: ...
       # adjust eta if fail
       if fail%10==0 eta *=0.8; if accepted eta=min(1.5,eta*1.1)
```

- mix with reverse or exponential.
- crossover: a=(1-λ)a+λ other.

Stable LSE:
```
def _lse_and_p(b, beta):
 L=b.size
 if L==0: return ones, 0
 m=b.max()
 if beta <=1: p=uniform; val=m
 else:
  x=beta*(b-m)
  # for stability, cap below -745
  x=np.maximum(x,-745)
  e=np.exp(x)
  s=e.sum()
  if s<=0: e=uniform; s=L
  p=e/s
  val=m+np.log(s)/beta
 return p, val
```
If beta large and b-m has large negative, underflow fine.

 if rho.size==0: return v/v.sum() if sum? Actually if all -inf? fallback.

 theta=(cssv[r]-1.0)/(r+1)
 w=np.maximum(v-theta,0)
 s=w.sum()
 if s<=... fallback uniform
 return w/s
```
Standard condition uses `1.0/(i+1)`? Let's verify. For projection onto simplex sum=1: find ρ max {ρ: u_ρ > (1 - sum_{j≤ρ}u_j)/ρ}? The common code:

cssv = np.cumsum(u)
rho = np.arange(1,n+1)
cond = u > (cssv - 1)/rho
hat = np.where(cond)[0].max()
theta = (cssv[hat] - 1)/(hat+1)
w = np.maximum(v - theta, 0)
```

```

`_smooth_perturb`:
```
def _smooth_perturb(a, rng, amp=0.35):
 z=rng.normal(size=a.size)
 # lowpass
 F=np.fft.rfft(z)
 cut=max(2, min(len(F), a.size//8 +2))
 F[cut:]=0
 f=np.fft.irfft(F,a.size)
 f=f-f.mean()
 sd=f.std()
 if sd>0: f=f/sd
 a2=a*np.exp(amp*f)
 return _normalize(a2)
```

Let's detail `local_refine` robust code.

```
def local_refine(a, deadline, rng, verbose=True, max_lp=8):
    a=_normalize(a)
    n=a.size
    if n < 64 or n > 4096:
       a=_resample(a, 512)
    bsize=_next_pow2(2*n-1)
    gsize=_next_pow2(3*n-2)
    b=_convolve(a)
    M=float(b.max()); score=2.0*n*M
    best_a=a.copy(); best_score=score; best_b=b.copy()
    beta_target=8.0
    eta=0.35
    topk=8
    fail=0; iter_no=0; no_improve=0; lp_count=0
    smooth_beta, smooth_val = _lse_and_p(b, beta_target/max(M,eps))
    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
       # update b/score of current
       if iter_no % 5 ==0 or True? We already compute after previous update; maintain b.
       if score < best_score - 1e-12:
           best_a=a.copy(); best_score=score; no_improve=0
       else:
           no_improve += 1
       if no_improve > 0 and no_improve % 50 ==0: pass
       # weights
       L=2*n-1
       K=min(L, max(4, topk + no_improve//200))
       # Use argpartition for top K (if K small). But b size L.
       if K==L:
          idx_all=None; vals=b
       else:
          idx=np.argpartition(b, -K)[-K:]
          vals=b[idx]
       beta=min(1e6, beta_target / max(M,1e-12))
       if K==L:
          p=np.exp(beta*(b-M)); s=p.sum(); p=p/s if s>0 else uniform
       else:
          p=np.zeros(L)
          ve=beta*(vals-M); ve=np.maximum(ve,-745); pe=np.exp(ve)
          ps=pe.sum()
          if ps<=0: pe=np.ones(K); ps=K
          p[idx]=pe/ps
       grad=_grad_from_weights(a,p,gsize,n)
       grad_c=grad - np.mean(grad)
       gmax=float(np.max(np.abs(grad_c)))
       if gmax <= 1e-14:
          # perturb
          a=_smooth_perturb(best_a,rng,0.35)
          b=_convolve(a); M=...; score=...
          fail=0; beta_target=max(6,beta_target*0.7); iter_no+=1; continue
       # Bregman update with line search on smooth
       d=grad_c / (np.std(a)+gmax*0.2) # scale
       # try multiple eta
       candidates=[]
       for mult in (eta, eta*1.5, eta*0.5, eta*0.25):
          a2=a * np.exp(-mult*d)
          a2=_normalize(a2)
          # maybe ensure finite
          b2=_convolve(a2)
          s2, p2? Need smooth value with same beta? We can compute `_lse_value(b2,beta)`
          sm2=_lse_value(b2,beta)
          candidates.append((sm2,a2,b2))
       choose min sm2; if sm2 < smooth_val - 1e-12 or best possible:
          a=best; b=b2; M=max; score=2nM
          # if update, update smooth_val=sm2
          eta=min(1.0, eta*1.05); fail=0
       else:
          # Euclidean line search
          d2=grad_c/gmax
          steps=[eta*0.3/a.size? Wait d2 sup1. step unit mass mean.
```
Let's think step scaling. For Bregman, `d` maybe large. Need choose. Compute `d_scale = np.mean(np.abs(grad_c))`? Bregman formula optimal for KL uses step in gradient per a. Since objective scale: b~1/n, score~1. Gradient size O(1/n). If d=grad_c/(std(a)+...), std(a)~1/√n? Actually a_i~1/n, std maybe c/n? For smooth density, values around 1/n, relative variations O(1), so std~1/n. grad~1/n. Ratio O(1). exp(-eta*d) with eta~0.5 changes by factor e^{±0.5}. Reasonable. Use `d = grad_c / max(1e-12, np.mean(np.abs(a)) + 1e-12)`? Wait mean(a)=1/n. grad_c magnitude: for active top p, each term p_k a ~ (1/K)*(1/n). Sum over K p? derivative =2 Σ_{k} p_k a_{...}. If p normalized over K top, each p ~1/K, terms ~1/(K n), number K -> /n? grad ~1/n. mean(a)=1/n, ratio O(1). Good. Use `scale = max(np.mean(a), 1e-12)`. Then d=grad_c/scale. eta ~0.1-1.0. Good. Euclidean step: use `step = eta * scale` times d_unit? If d_unit=grad_c/gmax, scale=mean(a), step=eta*scale. Good.

Line search for Bregman: 4 candidates each requires conv. Could be heavy. We can test just one or two: mult = eta, if fail use 0.5. But better performance. Let's do 3: eta, eta*0.5, eta*2 maybe if accepted previously. To save, compute b2 via conv (FFT) and LSE value. For n=256 okay.

If smooth accepted, set `smooth_val=sm2` using same beta? But if beta_target changed next iter. Fine.
If no candidate improves smooth:
- fail +=1
- reduce eta *=0.8
- every 10 failures reduce beta_target = max(4, beta_target*0.8)
- if fail>60:
    a = _resample? or perturb best; reset.
- if no_improve>300 and n<=2048 and lp_count<max_lp: LP direction.

LP direction code:
```
def _lp_direction(a, active_idx, ub):
    n=a.size; K=active_idx.size
    A=[]
    for k in active_idx:
       row=np.zeros(n)
       lo=max(0,k-n+1); hi=min(n-1,k)
       row[lo:hi+1] = 2.0 * a[k-lo:k-hi]?? Need index. For j in lo..hi, a_{k-j}. If j array, k-j = k-lo..k-hi. `row[lo:hi+1] = 2.0*a[k-hi:k-lo+1]`? Because when j=lo, k-lo; j=hi, k-hi, decreasing. Can we use the SOTA LP more strongly? The previous SOTA state of art described solving LP: fixed f, maximize sum g subject to f*g ≤ M. That is exactly our LP direction but for entire next g, not small d. Let's implement that as an update (not just direction). Given current a and M, solve LP to find a (g) with high sum but f*g≤M. Then rescale g to sum1, mix into a. This may make bigger jumps and enforce constraints. It is the SOTA approach. Let's implement rather than linearized direction. Primal LP variables g length n, objective maximize sum g, constraints A_k g ≤ M for k in active? Or all 2n-1? If all constraints, g can be found. But all dense. We can solve with `linprog`.

Primal:
max c^T g, c=ones(n)
A g ≤ M*ones(K)
g ≥0
where A_k_i=f_{k-i} if valid. For fixed f. If solve with all K=2n-1, objective limited. The optimum extreme point. We can then KKT update. This LP has n variables, up to 2n-1 constraints. For n=256, 511 constraints dense 131k, okay. For n=512, 1M constraints/vars 4MB, maybe okay but HiGHS maybe slower. For n=1024, 4M, 32MB, maybe okay. We can call few times.

This LP is convex and can produce a direction. We can implement `_lp_next(a, M, n_vars, max_cons)`. Use all constraints or active subset? SOTA says all constraints (active set binding). With all, M fixed to current max. If g feasible with sum > sum a? Since a itself feasible (f*f≤M) and sum=1, max sum≥1. It may find g with sum significantly >1 if constraints allow. Rescale to sum1. Update a=(1-t)a+t g for small t; maybe score decreases? Need line search/t. This is more expensive than linearized. But could be a powerful operator.

Let's derive dual maybe easier and cheaper: Find y≥0 (length K) minimizing ∑ y subject to A^T y ≥ 1. If dual feasible, ∑ g ≤ M∑y. But not direction. The primal g tells direction.

Implement primal with active constraints only? If use top K peaks with M, might ignore other peaks causing next score increase. Use all constraints for safety. 7. `_weighted_grad(a, w, n)`:
```
w=np.asarray(w,float).ravel()
n=a.size
if n==0 or w.size==0: return np.zeros(n)
gsize=_next_pow2(3*n-2)
ar=a[::-1]
A=np.fft.rfft(ar,gsize)
W=np.fft.rfft(w,gsize)
conv=np.fft.irfft(A*W,gsize).real
h=conv[n-1:2*n-1]
h=np.where(h>0,h,0) # derivative weights nonnegative so should >=0
return 2.0*h
```

19. `_lp_sota_update(a)`:
```
def _lp_sota_update(a):
  a=_normalize(a); n=a.size
  if n<8 or n>4096: return None
  b=_convolve_full(a); M=max
  if M<=0 return None
  if n <= 800:
      idx=np.arange(2*n-1)
  else:
      K=512
      if K > 2*n-1: idx=all
      else: idx=np.argpartition(b, -K)[-K:]
  # Build A
  A=np.zeros((idx.size,n), float)
  for r,k in enumerate(idx):
      lo=max(0, k-n+1); hi=min(n-1,k)
      if hi>=lo:
         # row for j lo..hi: a[k-j]
         A[r, lo:hi+1] = a[k-hi:k-lo+1][::-1]
  c=-np.ones(n)
  b_ub=M*np.ones(idx.size)
  bounds=[(0.0,None)]*n
  try:
    res=linprog(c, A_ub=A, b_ub=b_ub, bounds=bounds, method='highs', options={'disp':False})
  except Exception:
    return None
  if not res.success or res.x is None: return None
  g=np.asarray(res.x,float)
  g=np.nan_to_num(g, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
  g=np.maximum(g,0.0)
  s=g.sum()
  if s<=1e-12: return None
  g/=s
  # Try mix t
  best_a=None; best_score=score_of(a)
  for t in (0.05,0.1,0.2,0.35,0.5):
      a2=_normalize((1-t)*a + t*g)
      sc=_score_of(a2)
      if sc<best_score:
         best_score=sc; best_a=a2
  return best_a, best_score
```

def _get_given_sequence():
    seq = globals().get('height_sequence_1', None)
    if seq is None: return None
    try: arr=np.asarray(seq,dtype=float).ravel()
    except: return None
    arr=np.nan_to_num(arr,...); arr=max(arr,0)
    s=arr.sum()
    if arr.size==0 or s<=0: return None
    return arr/s

def propose_candidate(seed=0, budget_s=2400, **kwargs):
  t0=...
  budget=...
  deadline=t0+budget-30
  rng=...
  given=...
  # best
  if given is not None:
      best_a = _normalize(given)
      if best_a.size < 128: best_a=_resample(best_a,256)
      elif best_a.size > 2048: best_a=_resample(best_a,1024)
  else: best_a=_make_exponential(256,1.0)
  best_a=_normalize(best_a)
  best_score=_score_of(best_a)
  print
  # Maybe if best_score > 1.8, use lowdim to find better? do.
  # 1. local refine best for 250 sec
  t_end=min(deadline-1200, now+250)
  r=_local_refine(best_a.copy(), t_end, rng, max_lp=2 if best_a.size<=512 else 0)
  update
  # validate if <1.51 maybe
  # 2. SOTA LP loop from best at 256/512 for 150s
  if now < deadline-900:
      n_sota = best_a.size if 128<=best_a.size<=512 else 256
      a_sota = _resample(best_a, n_sota)
      r=_sota_loop(a_sota, min(deadline-900, now+150), rng, max_iters=50)
      update
  # 3. lowdim search 256 and 512
  if now < deadline-700:
      la=_lowdim_search(256, min(150, deadline-now-700), rng, seed)
      if la: update
  if now < deadline-500:
      la=_lowdim_search(512, min(120, deadline-now-500), rng, seed+1)
      update
  # 4. refine lowdim results / resampled best at 256/512/1024/2048
  for n in (256,512,1024,2048):
      avail = deadline - now
      if avail < 80: break
      if n == best_a.size:
         init=best_a.copy()
      else:
         init=_resample(best_a,n)
      sec= ... budget: 100 for 256, 120 for 512, 180 for 1024, 240 for 2048 scaled by remaining
      if sec>60:
         r=_local_refine(init, min(deadline-200, now+sec), rng, max_lp=1 if n<=512 else 0)
         update
      # also from lowdim best? Keep separate lowdim arrays? We can store best per n? Simpler use best global.
  # 5. multi-start perturb best length
  if now < deadline-200:
      for i in range(3):
         init=_random_perturb(best_a,rng,0.4)
         sec=... 60
         r=_local_refine(init,...)
  # 6. final deep refine
  if now < deadline-30:
      r=_local_refine(best_a.copy(), deadline-10, rng, max_lp=0)
      update
  # final validate: maybe try trimming/normalization
  out=best_a; out_score=_score_of(out)
  # Try resampling best to 256/512 and if score lower? Could be discrete artifact; if lower, update.
  for n in (256,512,1024):
      cand=_resample(out,n); sc=_score_of(cand)
      if sc<out_score: out=cand; out_score=sc
  # validate with evaluate_sequence
  try:
     ev=evaluate_sequence([float(x) for x in out])
     if np.isfinite(ev) and ev < out_score: out_score=ev
  except: pass
  return [float(x) for x in out]

Now, `_local_refine` code in detail.

```
def _local_refine(a, deadline, rng, max_lp=1, verbose=False):
    a = _normalize(a)
    n = a.size
    if n < 96:
        a = _resample(a, 256)
    if n > 4096:
        a = _resample(a, 4096)
    n = a.size
    b = _convolve_full(a)
    M = float(np.max(b)) if b.size else 0.0
    score = 2.0 * float(n) * M
    best_a = a.copy()
    best_score = score
    if M <= 0: M = 1e-300
    beta_target = 8.0
    eta = 0.45
    topk = 6
    fail = 0
    no_improve = 0
    it = 0
    lp_used = 0
    smooth_val = None
    beta = min(1e6, beta_target/max(M,1e-12))
    _, smooth_val = _lse_and_p(b,beta)
    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        if score < best_score - 1e-13:
            best_a = a.copy()
            best_score = score
            no_improve = 0
        else:
            # no_improve increments only occasionally? Every loop maybe too fast reset beta.
            no_improve += 1
        if no_improve > 1000:
            no_improve = 500 # prevent overflow? okay
        K = min(2*n-1, topk + max(0, no_improve)//120)
        beta = min(1e6, beta_target/max(M,1e-12))
        if K <= 0: K=1
        # compute p
        L = 2*n-1
        if K >= L:
           p = _lse_and_p(b,beta)[0]
        else:
           idx = np.argpartition(b, -K)[-K:]
           vals = b[idx]
           w = beta*(vals-M)
           w = np.maximum(w,-745.0)
           e = np.exp(w)
           s = float(e.sum())
           p = np.zeros(L)
           if s > 0:
              p[idx] = e/s
           else:
              p = _lse_and_p(b,beta)[0]
        grad = _weighted_grad(a,p,n)
        grad_c = grad - np.mean(grad)
        gmax = float(np.max(np.abs(grad_c))) if grad_c.size else 0.0
        if gmax <= 1e-14:
            a = _random_perturb best
            b = _convolve_full(a)
            M = max
            score = 2nM
            fail=0; beta_target=max(6,beta_target*0.7); eta=0.45
            it +=1
            continue
        scale = max(float(np.mean(a)), 1e-12)
        d = grad_c / scale
        # Bregman line search
        cand_best=None; cand_best_sm=inf
        # use 3 muls
        for mult in (eta, 0.5*eta, 1.5*eta):
            a2 = a * np.exp(-mult*d)
            a2 = np.nan_to_num(a2, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
            a2 = np.maximum(a2,0.0)
            ssum = a2.sum()
            if ssum <= 0 or not finite: continue
            a2 /= ssum
            b2 = _convolve_full(a2)
            m2=float(np.max(b2))
            sc2=2*n*m2
            sm2=_lse_value(b2,beta)
            if cand_best is None or sm2 < cand_best_sm:
                cand_best=(sm2, sc2, a2.copy(), b2, m2)
        accepted=False
        if cand_best is not None and cand_best[0] < smooth_val - 1e-13:
            smooth_val, sc2, a_new, b2, m2 = cand_best
            # Optionally require sc2 not exploding
            if sc2 < best_score + 0.2:
                a=a_new; b=b2; M=m2; score=sc2
                accepted=True
                fail=0
                eta=min(1.2, eta*1.08)
                if score < best_score:
                    beta_target=min(80.0,beta_target*1.05)
                    topk=min(256, topk+1)
                # smooth_val from accepted beta? okay
            else:
                fail +=1
        if not accepted:
            # Euclidean
            d2=grad_c/gmax
            step_base=eta*scale
            eu_valid=False
            # Try f 1,0.5,2
            for f in (1.0,0.5,2.0):
                a2=_project_simplex(a - f*step_base*d2)
                b2=_convolve_full(a2)
                m2=float(np.max(b2)); sc2=2*n*m2
                sm2=_lse_value(b2,beta)
                if cand_best is None or sm2 < cand_best[0]:
                    cand_best=(sm2,sc2,a2.copy(),b2,m2)
            if cand_best is not None and cand_best[0] < smooth_val - 1e-13 and cand_best[1] < best_score + 0.2:
                smooth_val, sc2, a_new, b2, m2 = cand_best
                a=a_new; b=b2; M=m2; score=sc2
                fail=0
                # eta adjust maybe no
            else:
                fail += 1
                eta *= 0.85
                if fail % 20 == 0:
                    beta_target = max(4.0, beta_target*0.85)
        if fail > 80:
            a = _random_perturb(best_a, rng, 0.35)
            b = _convolve_full(a)
            M = max; score=...
            fail=0; beta_target=max(6.0,beta_target*0.6); eta=0.45
            topk=max(6, topk//2)
        # LP update
        if max_lp >0 and lp_used<max_lp and n<=512 and no_improve>200 and (deadline - time.monotonic() > 30):
            _lp_sota_update(a)
            if res:
              lp_used +=1
              a2, sc2=res
              if sc2 < best_score:
                  best_a=a2.copy(); best_score=sc2
                  a=a2.copy()
              else:
                  # maybe set current to a mix? If not improve, still can set to a2? It might be feasible. Perhaps set current to a2 to explore.
                  # But if sc2 > score, could waste. Use if sc2 < score + 0.05? Let's set to perturbed best instead.
                  pass
              b=_convolve_full(a); M=...; score=...
              no_improve=0
              fail=0
        it +=1
        if verbose and it % 1000 ==0:
            print(...)
    if score < best_score: best update
    return best_a, best_score
```

def propose_candidate(seed=0, budget_s=2400, **kwargs):
    t0=time.monotonic()
    try: budget=float(budget_s)
    except: budget=2400.0
    if budget <=0 or not np.isfinite(budget): budget=2400.0
    deadline=t0+min(budget,2400.0)-25.0
    rng=np.random.default_rng(int(seed))
    given=_get_given_sequence()
    if given is not None and given.size>0:
       base_len = given.size
       if base_len < 128: base_len=256
       elif base_len > 2048: base_len=1024
       best_a=_resample(given, base_len)
    else:
       best_a=_make_exponential(256,1.0)
    best_a=_normalize(best_a)
    best_score=_score_of(best_a)
    print(...)
    # function update? Use local variables.
    # local refine 1
    if time.monotonic() < deadline - 1000:
       t_end = min(deadline - 1000, time.monotonic() + 240.0)
       res_a,res_score=_local_refine(best_a.copy(), t_end, rng, max_lp=2 if best_a.size<=512 else 0, verbose=True)
       if finite and < best: update
    # SOTA loop
    if time.monotonic() < deadline - 800:
       n_sota = best_a.size if 128<=best_a.size<=512 else 256
       sota_init = _resample(best_a, n_sota)
       t_end = min(deadline - 800, time.monotonic() + 160.0)
       res_a,res_score=_sota_loop(sota_init, t_end, rng, max_iters=40)
       update
    # lowdim
    if time.monotonic() < deadline - 650:
       t_end = min(deadline - 650, time.monotonic() + 160.0)
       seq=_lowdim_search(256, t_end, rng, seed)
       if seq is not None: sc=_score_of(seq); update
    if time.monotonic() < deadline - 450:
       t_end = min(deadline - 450, time.monotonic() + 120.0)
       seq=_lowdim_search(512, t_end, rng, seed+1)
       update
    # refine lengths
    for n in (256,512,1024,2048):
       rem=deadline-time.monotonic()
       if rem < 80: break
       if n == best_a.size:
          init=best_a.copy()
       else:
          init=_resample(best_a,n)
       if n<=256: sec=100
       elif n<=512: sec=120
       elif n<=1024: sec=180
       else: sec=220
       sec=min(sec-20, rem-180)
       if sec < 30: sec=20
       t_end=time.monotonic()+sec

       update

    # multi-start from best length a few perturbations
    for i in range(4):
       rem=deadline-time.monotonic()
       if rem < 90: break
       init=_random_perturb(best_a,rng,0.45)
       sec=min(70.0, rem-120.0)
       if sec<20: sec=20
       res_a,res_score=_local_refine(init, time.monotonic()+sec, rng, max_lp=0)
       update
    # final deep refine
    rem=deadline-time.monotonic()
    if rem > 30:
       res_a,res_score=_local_refine(best_a.copy(), deadline-10, rng, max_lp=0)
       update
    # final try resampling best to small lengths to see if score improves
    final_a=best_a.copy(); final_score=best_score
    for n in (128,256,512,1024,2048):
       cand=_resample(final_a,n)
       sc=_score_of(cand)
       if finite and sc<final_score:
          final_a=cand; final_score=sc
    out=np.asarray(final_a,float).ravel(); out=... normalize
    # optional external validation
    ext=_external_score([float(x) for x in out])
    if ext is not None and np.isfinite(ext):
       print external
    # If final sequence length >4096, resample
    return [float(x) for x in out]

def _lowdim_search(n, deadline, rng, seed):
    n=int(n)
    if deadline - time.monotonic() < 20 or differential_evolution is None:
       return _make_exponential(n,1.0)
    bounds=[(-4.0,4.0) for _ in range(10)]
    best_seq=None; best_score=1e100
    de_iter = ... based on remaining and n
    try:
       res=differential_evolution(... maxiter=de_iter, popsize=10 ...)
       if res.x is not None:
          seq=_params_to_sequence(res.x,n); sc=_score_of(seq)
          if sc<best_score: best_seq=seq; best_score=sc
          de_x=res.x.copy()
    except: de_x=None
    # nelder starts
    starts=[]
    if de_x is not None: starts.append(de_x)
    starts.append(np.array([0,-2,0,0,0,0,0,0,0,0])) # exponential-ish
    starts.append(np.array([0,-1,1,-1,0.5,-0.5,0,0,1,-1]))
    for i in range(max(0, min(8, int((deadline-time.monotonic())/25)))):
       xrng = np.empty(10)
       for j in range(10): xrng[j]=rng.uniform(-3,3)
       starts.append(xrng)
    for x0 in starts:
       if time.monotonic() > deadline - 10: break
       if minimize is None: break
       try:
          res=minimize(_param_objective, x0, args=(n, True), method='Nelder-Mead', options={'maxiter':150,'maxfev':400,'xatol':1e-5,'fatol':1e-6})
          if res.x is not None:
             seq=...; sc=...
             update
       except: pass
    if best_seq is None: best_seq=_params_to_sequence(starts[0],n)
    return _normalize(best_seq)

`_lse_and_p`:
```
def _lse_and_p(b,beta):
 b=np.asarray(b,float).ravel()
 L=b.size
 if L==0: return np.ones(0),0.0
 m=float(np.max(b))
 if beta <=1.0:
    p=np.full(L, 1.0/L)
    return p,m
 x=beta*(b-m)
 x=np.maximum(x,-745.0)
 e=np.exp(x)
 s=float(e.sum())
 if s<=0 or not finite:
    p=np.full(L,1.0/L); return p,m
 p=e/s
 val=m+float(np.log(s))/beta
 return p,val
```
`_lse_value` similar.

`_weighted_grad`:

w=np.asarray(w,float).ravel()
if w.size==0 or a.size==0: return zeros
n=a.size
gsize=_next_pow2(3*n-2)
ar=a[::-1]
A=np.fft.rfft(ar,gsize)
W=np.fft.rfft(w,gsize)
conv=np.fft.irfft(A*W,gsize).real
h=conv[n-1:2*n-1]
h=np.maximum(h,0.0)
return 2.0*h
```
If w length not 2n-1? we pass. If p includes zeros outside? length full.

`_project_simplex`: code with cond. Let's test all zeros: cond false, fallback uniform.
```
def _project_simplex(v):
 v=np.asarray(v,float).ravel()
 n=v.size
 if n==0: return v
 s=float(np.sum(v))
 if s <= 0 or not np.isfinite(s): return np.full(n,1.0/n)
 u=np.sort(v)[::-1]
 cssv=np.cumsum(u)
 idxs=np.arange(1,n+1,dtype=float)
 cond = u > (cssv - 1.0) / idxs
 if np.any(cond):
    r=int(np.max(np.where(cond)[0]))
    theta=(cssv[r]-1.0)/(r+1.0)
    w=np.maximum(v-theta,0.0)
 else:
    # if all below? Could simply shift to mean
    w=np.maximum(v - (s/n - 1.0/max(n,1)), 0.0) # not robust
    if np.sum(w)<=0: w=np.ones(n)
 sw=float(np.sum(w))
 if sw <= 1e-300 or not finite:
    w=np.ones(n); sw=float(n)
 return w/sw
```
If v sum<1 and all negative, cond maybe false, fallback w maybe max(v - (s/n-1)) likely 1, uniform. Good.

`_make_exponential` okay.

`_resample`: If old.size == new_n return copy. If old.size==0. Use `np.linspace` maybe for large okay.
```
def _resample(old,new_n):
 old=np.asarray(old,float).ravel(); old=_normalize(old)
 new_n=int(new_n)
 if new_n <1: new_n=1
 if old.size ==0: return np.ones(new_n)/new_n
 if old.size == new_n: return old.copy()
 if old.size ==1: return np.full(new_n,1.0/new_n)
 x_old=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,old.size)
 x_new=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,new_n)
 new=np.interp(x_new,x_old,old)
 return _normalize(new)
```

Perturb:
```
def _smooth_perturb(a,rng,amp=0.35):
 a=_normalize(a); n=a.size
 z=rng.normal(size=n)
 F=np.fft.rfft(z)
 cut=max(2, min(len(F), n//8 +2))
 if cut < len(F): F[cut:]=0.0
 f=np.fft.irfft(F,n).real
 f=f-f.mean()
 sd=float(f.std())
 if sd<=1e-12: f=rng.normal(size=n); f=f-f.mean(); sd=f.std()
 if sd>0: f=f/sd
 a2=a*np.exp(amp*f)
 return _normalize(a2)
```
If FFT length n? `rfft` of n returns len n//2+1. Good. `irfft(F,n)`.

`_block_perturb`:

n=a.size; c=a.copy(); width=max(2,n//20); start=int(rng.integers(0,max(1,n-width+1))); c[start:start+width] *= np.exp(rng.normal(0,strength)); return normalize

def _params_to_sequence(params,n):

 if params.size < B.shape[1]: pad? not needed.

 logf = np.nan_to_num(logf, nan=-700.0, posinf=700.0, neginf=-700.0)
 logf -= float(np.max(logf))
 a=np.exp(logf)
 a=np.maximum(a,0.0)
 return _normalize(a)

def _param_objective(params,n,use_smooth=True):

 b=_convolve_full(a)
 if b.size==0: return 1e100
 mb=float(np.max(b))
 if mb <=0: return 1e100
 if not use_smooth:
   return 2.0*a.size*mb
 beta=min(1e6, 60.0/max(mb,1e-12))
 return 2.0*a.size*_lse_value(b,beta)

def _lowdim_search(n, deadline, rng, seed):
 n=int(n)
 rem0=deadline-time.monotonic()
 if rem0 < 30 or differential_evolution is None and minimize is None:
    return _make_exponential(n,1.0)
 bounds=[(-4.0,4.0) for _ in range(10)]
 best_seq=None; best_score=1e100; de_x=None
 if differential_evolution is not None and time.monotonic() < deadline - 60:
    if n <= 256: de_iter=max(25, int(rem0/8))
    elif n <= 512: de_iter=max(18, int(rem0/10))
    else: de_iter=max(12, int(rem0/12))
    de_iter=min(80, max(10, de_iter))
    try:
       res=differential_evolution(_param_objective, bounds, args=(n, True), maxiter=de_iter, popsize=10, tol=0.02, atol=1e-5, seed=int(seed) if finite else 0, polish=False, init='sobol', workers=1)
       if res is not None and res.x is not None:
          seq=_params_to_sequence(res.x,n); sc=_score_of(seq)
          if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
             best_seq=seq; best_score=sc; de_x=np.asarray(res.x,float).copy()
    except Exception:
       pass
 starts=[]
 if de_x is not None:
    starts.append(de_x.copy())
 base1=np.zeros(10); base1[1]=-2.0; starts.append(base1)
 base2=np.zeros(10); base2[1]=-1.0; base2[3]=1.0; base2[4]=0.8; base2[9]=-1.2; starts.append(base2)

    if time.monotonic() > deadline - 5: break
    if minimize is None: break

          if finite and sc<best_score: ...
    except: pass

    best_seq=_params_to_sequence(starts[0],n)
 return _normalize(best_seq)

`_lp_sota_update`:

def _lp_sota_update(a):
 if linprog is None: return None
 a=_normalize(a); n=a.size
 if n < 16 or n > 1024: return None
 b=_convolve_full(a); M=float(np.max(b))
 if M<=0: return None
 if n <= 700:
    idx=np.arange(2*n-1)
 else:
    K=min(512,2*n-1)
    idx=np.argpartition(b,-K)[-K:]
 A=np.empty((idx.size,n),float)
 A[:] = 0.0
 for r in range(idx.size):
    k=int(idx[r])
    lo=max(0,k-n+1); hi=min(n-1,k)
    if hi >= lo:
       A[r, lo:hi+1] = a[k-hi:k-lo+1][::-1]
 c=-np.ones(n)
 b_ub=(M*(1.0+1e-9)+1e-12)*np.ones(idx.size)
 bounds=[(0.0,None)]*n
 try:
    res=linprog(c, A_ub=A, b_ub=b_ub, bounds=bounds, method='highs', options={'disp':False})
 except Exception:
    return None
 if res is None or not getattr(res,'success',False): return None
 g=np.asarray(res.x,float)
 g=np.nan_to_num(g,...); g=np.maximum(g,0)
 s=float(g.sum())
 if s<=1e-12: return None
 g /= s
 best_a=None; best_score=_score_of(a)
 for t in (0.05,0.1,0.2,0.35,0.5):
    a2=_normalize((1.0-t)*a + t*g)
    sc=_score_of(a2)
    if finite and sc < best_score:
       best_score=sc; best_a=a2
 if best_a is None: return None
 return best_a, float(best_score)

def _sota_loop(a, deadline, rng, max_iters=50):
 a=_normalize(a); n=a.size
 if n<16 or n>1024 or linprog is None:
    return a, _score_of(a)
 score=_score_of(a); best_a=a.copy(); best_score=score; fails=0
 for it in range(int(max_iters)):
    if time.monotonic() > deadline: break
    res=_lp_sota_update(a)
    if res is not None:
       a2, sc=res
       if sc < best_score:
          best_a=a2.copy(); best_score=sc
       if sc < score + 1e-12:
          a=a2.copy(); score=sc; fails=0
       else:
          fails += 1
    else:
       fails += 1
       if fails > 5:
          a=_random_perturb(best_a, rng, 0.25)
          score=_score_of(a)
          fails=0
    if best_score < 1.5027: break
 return best_a,best_score

Pseudo full code (write carefully):
```
def _local_refine(a, deadline, rng, max_lp=1, verbose=False):
    a=_normalize(a)
    if a.size < 96: a=_resample(a,256)
    if a.size > 4096: a=_resample(a,4096)
    n=a.size
    b=_convolve_full(a)
    M=float(np.max(b)) if b.size else 0.0
    if M <= 1e-300: M=1e-300
    score=2.0*float(n)*M
    best_a=a.copy(); best_score=score
    beta_target=8.0
    eta=0.45
    topk=6
    fail=0; no_improve=0; it=0; lp_used=0
    beta=min(1e6, beta_target/M)
    _, smooth_val = _lse_and_p(b,beta)
    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
       if score < best_score - 1e-12:
          best_a=a.copy(); best_score=score; no_improve=0
       else:
          no_improve += 1
          if no_improve > 5000: no_improve=5000
       K = int(min(2*n-1, max(4, topk + no_improve//100)))
       beta = min(1e6, beta_target/M)
       L=2*n-1
       if K >= L:
          p = _lse_and_p(b,beta)[0]
       else:
          idx = np.argpartition(b, -K)[-K:]
          vals = b[idx]
          w = beta*(vals - M)
          w = np.maximum(w, -745.0)
          e = np.exp(w)
          se = float(e.sum())
          if se > 0 and np.isfinite(se):
             p = np.zeros(L)
             p[idx] = e/se
          else:
             p = _lse_and_p(b,beta)[0]
       grad = _weighted_grad(a,p,n)
       grad_c = grad - float(np.mean(grad))
       gmax = float(np.max(np.abs(grad_c))) if grad_c.size else 0.0
       if gmax <= 1e-14:
          a = _random_perturb(best_a, rng, 0.35)
          b = _convolve_full(a); M=...; score=...
          beta = min(...); _, smooth_val = _lse_and_p(b,beta)
          fail=0; beta_target=max(6,beta_target*0.7); eta=0.45; topk=max(6,topk//2)
          it +=1
          if verbose and it%1000==0: print(...)
          continue
       scale=max(float(np.mean(a)),1e-12)
       d=grad_c/scale
       breg_cands=[]
       for mult in (eta, 0.5*eta, 1.5*eta):
          a2=a*np.exp(-mult*d)
          a2=np.nan_to_num(a2, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
          a2=np.maximum(a2,0.0)
          ssum=float(a2.sum())
          if ssum > 1e-300 and np.isfinite(ssum):
             a2= a2/ssum
             b2=_convolve_full(a2)
             m2=float(np.max(b2)) if b2.size else 0.0
             sc2=2.0*float(n)*m2
             sm2=_lse_value(b2,beta)
             breg_cands.append((sm2,sc2,a2.copy(),b2,m2))
       accepted=False
       item=_select_candidate(breg_cands)
       if item is not None and item[0] < smooth_val - 1e-12 and item[1] < score + 0.1:
          a=item[2]; b=item[3]; M=item[4]; score=item[1]; smooth_val=item[0]
          accepted=True; fail=0
          eta=min(1.25, eta*1.07)
          if score < best_score:
             beta_target=min(90.0, beta_target*1.05)
             topk=min(256, topk+1)
       if not accepted:
          d2=grad_c/gmax
          step=eta*scale
          eu_cands=[]
          for f in (1.0,0.5,2.0):
             a2=_project_simplex(a - f*step*d2)
             b2=_convolve_full(a2); m2=...; sc2=...; sm2=...
             eu_cands.append(...)
          item=_select_candidate(eu_cands)
          if item is not None and item[0] < smooth_val - 1e-12 and item[1] < score + 0.1:
             a=item[2]; b=item[3]; M=item[4]; score=item[1]; smooth_val=item[0]
             fail=0
             # eta maybe unchanged
          else:
             fail +=1
             eta *=0.85
             if fail % 20 ==0:
                beta_target=max(4.0,beta_target*0.85)
       if fail > 80:
          a=_random_perturb(best_a,rng,0.4)
          b=_convolve_full(a); M=...; score=...
          beta=min(...); _,smooth_val=_lse_and_p(b,beta)
          fail=0; beta_target=max(6.0,beta_target*0.6); eta=0.45; topk=max(6,topk//2)
       if max_lp >0 and lp_used < max_lp and n <= 512 and no_improve > 250 and (deadline - time.monotonic()) > 35:
          res=_lp_sota_update(a)
          if res is not None:
             a2, sc2=res
             lp_used +=1
             if sc2 < best_score:
                best_a=a2.copy(); best_score=sc2
             if sc2 <= score + 1e-9:
                a=a2.copy()
             # else keep
             b=_convolve_full(a); M=...; score=...
             beta=min(...); _,smooth_val=_lse_and_p(b,beta)
             no_improve=0; fail=0
       it +=1
       if verbose and (it % 2000 == 0 or it==1):
          print(f"local n={n} it={it} score={score:.8f} best={best_score:.8f} beta_t={beta_target:.1f} eta={eta:.3f} fail={fail}", flush=True)
    if score < best_score:
       best_a=a.copy(); best_score=score
    if verbose:
       print(...)
    return best_a, float(best_score)
```

```python
"""
Search for low-peak nonnegative self-convolutions.

The evaluator minimizes:
    score(a) = 2 * n * max(a * a) / (sum(a))^2,
where the convolution is the full linear convolution and a_i >= 0.
The objective is scale invariant, so search is performed on the simplex
sum(a)=1.

This program combines:
  * a reusable construction from the global height_sequence_1 if present,
  * low-dimensional Fourier/Gaussian parametrization with derivative-free
    global search,
  * full-sequence projected/Bregman gradient descent on a log-sum-exp
    smoothing of max(a*a),
  * consecutive active-peak subgradient steps,
  * a convex LP "SOTA" update:
        maximize sum(g)  s.t.  (a*g)_k <= max(a*a), g_i >= 0,
    followed by a small mixing/line search,
  * random smooth/block/sine perturbations and resampling across lengths.

All helper functions are top-level and do not use nested functions or lambdas.
"""

import time
import numpy as np

try:
    from scipy.optimize import differential_evolution, minimize, linprog
except Exception:
    differential_evolution = None
    minimize = None
    linprog = None

np.seterr(over="ignore", under="ignore", invalid="ignore", divide="ignore")

_BASIS_CACHE = {}


def _next_pow2(size):
    size = int(size)
    if size <= 1:
        return 1
    return 1 << (size - 1).bit_length()


def _normalize(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    a = np.nan_to_num(a, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    if a.size == 0:
        return np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return np.full(a.size, 1.0 / float(a.size), dtype=np.float64)
    return a / s


def _project_simplex(v):
    v = np.asarray(v, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = v.size
    if n == 0:
        return v
    s = float(v.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return np.full(n, 1.0 / float(n), dtype=np.float64)

    u = np.sort(v)[::-1]
    cssv = np.cumsum(u)
    idxs = np.arange(1, n + 1, dtype=np.float64)
    cond = u > (cssv - 1.0) / idxs

    if np.any(cond):
        r = int(np.max(np.where(cond)[0]))
        theta = (cssv[r] - 1.0) / float(r + 1)
        w = np.maximum(v - theta, 0.0)
    else:
        theta = float(np.mean(v) - 1.0 / float(n))
        w = np.maximum(v - theta, 0.0)

    sw = float(w.sum())
    if sw <= 1e-300 or not np.isfinite(sw):
        w = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
        sw = float(n)
    return w / sw


def _convolve_full(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.zeros(0, dtype=np.float64)

    if n <= 256:
        b = np.convolve(a, a, mode="full")
    else:
        m = _next_pow2(2 * n - 1)
        A = np.fft.rfft(a, m)
        b = np.fft.irfft(A * A, m)
        b = b[:2 * n - 1]

    b = np.asarray(b, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    b = np.nan_to_num(b, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    b = np.maximum(b, 0.0)
    return b


def _score_of(a):
    a = _normalize(a)
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return 1e100
    b = _convolve_full(a)
    if b.size == 0:
        return 1e100
    mb = float(np.max(b))
    if not np.isfinite(mb):
        return 1e100
    return float(2.0 * float(n) * mb)


def _lse_value(b, beta):
    b = np.asarray(b, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    L = b.size
    if L == 0:
        return 0.0
    m = float(np.max(b))
    if beta <= 1.0:
        return m
    x = beta * (b - m)
    x = np.maximum(x, -745.0)
    e = np.exp(x)
    s = float(e.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return m
    return m + float(np.log(s)) / beta


def _lse_and_p(b, beta):
    b = np.asarray(b, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    L = b.size
    if L == 0:
        return np.zeros(0, dtype=np.float64), 0.0
    m = float(np.max(b))
    if beta <= 1.0:
        return np.full(L, 1.0 / float(L), dtype=np.float64), m
    x = beta * (b - m)
    x = np.maximum(x, -745.0)
    e = np.exp(x)
    s = float(e.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300 or not np.isfinite(s):
        p = np.full(L, 1.0 / float(L), dtype=np.float64)
        return p, m
    p = e / s
    val = m + float(np.log(s)) / beta
    return p, val


def _weighted_grad(a, w, n):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    w = np.asarray(w, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = int(n)
    if n == 0 or w.size == 0:
        return np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

    gsize = _next_pow2(3 * n - 2)
    ar = a[::-1]
    A = np.fft.rfft(ar, gsize)
    W = np.fft.rfft(w, gsize)
    conv = np.fft.irfft(A * W, gsize).real
    h = conv[n - 1:2 * n - 1]
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=np.float64)
    h = np.nan_to_num(h, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    h = np.maximum(h, 0.0)
    return 2.0 * h


def _make_exponential(n, alpha):
    n = max(2, int(n))
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n, dtype=np.float64)
    a = np.exp(-float(alpha) * x)
    return _normalize(a)


def _resample(old, new_n):
    old = np.asarray(old, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    old = _normalize(old)
    new_n = max(1, int(new_n))

    if old.size == 0:
        return np.ones(new_n, dtype=np.float64) / float(new_n)
    if old.size == new_n:
        return old.copy()
    if old.size == 1:
        return np.full(new_n, 1.0 / float(new_n), dtype=np.float64)

    x_old = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, old.size, dtype=np.float64)
    x_new = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, new_n, dtype=np.float64)
    new = np.interp(x_new, x_old, old)
    return _normalize(new)


def _smooth_perturb(a, rng, amp=0.35):
    a = _normalize(a)
    n = a.size
    z = rng.normal(size=n)
    F = np.fft.rfft(z)
    cut = max(2, min(len(F), n // 8 + 2))
    if cut < len(F):
        F[cut:] = 0.0
    f = np.fft.irfft(F, n).real
    f = f - float(np.mean(f))
    sd = float(np.std(f))
    if sd <= 1e-12:
        f = rng.normal(size=n)
        f = f - float(np.mean(f))
        sd = float(np.std(f))
    if sd > 1e-12:
        f = f / sd
    a2 = a * np.exp(float(amp) * f)
    return _normalize(a2)


def _block_perturb(a, rng, strength=0.30):
    a = _normalize(a)
    n = a.size
    c = a.copy()
    width = max(2, n // 20)
    start = int(rng.integers(0, max(1, n - width + 1)))
    stop = min(n, start + width)
    c[start:stop] *= float(np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, float(strength))))
    return _normalize(c)


def _sin_perturb(a, rng, amp=0.25):
    a = _normalize(a)
    n = a.size
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n, dtype=np.float64)
    m = int(rng.integers(1, 5))
    ph = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi))
    mod = 1.0 + float(amp) * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * float(m) * x + ph)
    mod = np.maximum(mod, 0.05)
    return _normalize(a * mod)


def _random_perturb(a, rng, strength=0.35):
    a = _normalize(a)
    r = float(rng.random())
    if r < 0.35:
        return _smooth_perturb(a, rng, strength)
    if r < 0.60:
        return _block_perturb(a, rng, 0.8 * strength)
    if r < 0.85:
        return _sin_perturb(a, rng, 0.25)
    m = 0.2 + 0.5 * float(rng.random())
    a2 = (1.0 - m) * a + m * a[::-1]
    return _normalize(a2)


def _get_basis(n):
    n = max(2, int(n))
    if n in _BASIS_CACHE:
        return _BASIS_CACHE[n]

    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n, dtype=np.float64)
    B = np.empty((n, 10), dtype=np.float64)
    B[:, 0] = 1.0
    B[:, 1] = x
    B[:, 2] = (x - 0.5) ** 2
    B[:, 3] = (x - 0.5) ** 3
    B[:, 4] = np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * x)
    B[:, 5] = np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * x)
    B[:, 6] = np.sin(4.0 * np.pi * x)
    B[:, 7] = np.cos(4.0 * np.pi * x)
    B[:, 8] = np.exp(-((x - 0.25) / 0.18) ** 2)
    B[:, 9] = np.exp(-((x - 0.75) / 0.18) ** 2)

    _BASIS_CACHE[n] = B
    return B


def _params_to_sequence(params, n):
    n = max(2, int(n))
    B = _get_basis(n)
    params = np.asarray(params, dtype=np.float64).ravel()

    if params.size < B.shape[1]:
        tmp = np.zeros(B.shape[1], dtype=np.float64)
        tmp[:params.size] = params
        params = tmp
    elif params.size > B.shape[1]:
        params = params[:B.shape[1]]

    logf = B.dot(params)
    logf = np.nan_to_num(logf, nan=-700.0, posinf=700.0, neginf=-700.0)
    logf = logf - float(np.max(logf))
    a = np.exp(logf)
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    return _normalize(a)


def _param_objective(params, n, use_smooth):
    n = int(n)
    a = _params_to_sequence(params, n)
    b = _convolve_full(a)
    if b.size == 0:
        return 1e100
    mb = float(np.max(b))
    if mb <= 1e-300 or not np.isfinite(mb):
        return 1e100
    if not use_smooth:
        return float(2.0 * float(n) * mb)
    beta = min(1e6, 60.0 / max(mb, 1e-12))
    return float(2.0 * float(n) * _lse_value(b, beta))


def _choose_param_start(rng, seed):
    x = np.empty(10, dtype=np.float64)
    r = rng.default_rng(seed) if hasattr(rng, "default_rng") else rng
    for j in range(10):
        x[j] = float(r.uniform(-3.0, 3.0))
    return x


def _lowdim_search(n, deadline, rng, seed):
    n = max(128, int(n))
    rem0 = max(0.0, float(deadline - time.monotonic()))
    if rem0 < 30.0 or (differential_evolution is None and minimize is None):
        return _make_exponential(n, 1.0)

    bounds = []
    for _ in range(10):
        bounds.append((-4.0, 4.0))

    best_seq = None
    best_score = 1e100
    de_x = None

    if differential_evolution is not None and time.monotonic() < deadline - 45.0:
        if n <= 256:
            de_iter = max(25, int(rem0 / 8.0))
        elif n <= 512:
            de_iter = max(18, int(rem0 / 10.0))
        else:
            de_iter = max(12, int(rem0 / 12.0))
        de_iter = min(80, max(10, de_iter))

        try:
            res = differential_evolution(
                _param_objective,
                bounds,
                args=(n, True),
                maxiter=de_iter,
                popsize=10,
                tol=0.02,
                atol=1e-5,
                seed=int(seed) % (2 ** 31),
                polish=False,
                init="sobol",
                workers=1,
            )
            if res is not None and res.x is not None:
                seq = _params_to_sequence(res.x, n)
                sc = _score_of(seq)
                if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
                    best_seq = seq
                    best_score = float(sc)
                    de_x = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64).copy()
        except Exception:
            pass

    starts = []
    if de_x is not None:
        starts.append(de_x.copy())

    base1 = np.zeros(10, dtype=np.float64)
    base1[1] = -2.0
    starts.append(base1)

    base2 = np.zeros(10, dtype=np.float64)
    base2[1] = -1.0
    base2[3] = 1.0
    base2[4] = 0.8
    base2[9] = -1.2
    starts.append(base2)

    base3 = np.zeros(10, dtype=np.float64)
    base3[1] = 1.0
    base3[3] = -1.0
    base3[8] = -1.2
    starts.append(base3)

    extra = int(np.clip((deadline - time.monotonic()) / 25.0, 0, 6))
    for _ in range(extra):
        if time.monotonic() > deadline - 20.0:
            break
        starts.append(_choose_param_start(rng, int(seed) + np.random.randint(0, 100000)))

    if minimize is not None:
        for x0 in starts:
            if time.monotonic() > deadline - 10.0:
                break
            try:
                res = minimize(
                    _param_objective,
                    x0,
                    args=(n, True),
                    method="Nelder-Mead",
                    options={
                        "maxiter": 100,
                        "maxfev": 220,
                        "xatol": 1e-5,
                        "fatol": 1e-6,
                    },
                )
                if res is not None and res.x is not None:
                    seq = _params_to_sequence(res.x, n)
                    sc = _score_of(seq)
                    if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
                        best_seq = seq
                        best_score = float(sc)
            except Exception:
                pass

    if best_seq is None:
        if starts:
            best_seq = _params_to_sequence(starts[0], n)
        else:
            best_seq = _make_exponential(n, 1.0)
    return _normalize(best_seq)


def _lp_sota_update(a):
    if linprog is None:
        return None

    a = _normalize(a)
    n = a.size
    if n < 16 or n > 1024:
        return None

    b = _convolve_full(a)
    M = float(np.max(b)) if b.size else 0.0
    if M <= 1e-300 or not np.isfinite(M):
        return None

    L = 2 * n - 1
    idx = np.arange(L, dtype=np.int64)

    A = np.zeros((L, n), dtype=np.float64)
    for r in range(L):
        k = int(idx[r])
        lo = max(0, k - n + 1)
        hi = min(n - 1, k)
        if hi >= lo:
            A[r, lo:hi + 1] = a[k - hi:k - lo + 1][::-1]

    c = -np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    b_ub = (M * (1.0 + 1e-9) + 1e-12) * np.ones(L, dtype=np.float64)
    ub = max(1e-6, 50.0 / float(n))
    bounds = []
    for _ in range(n):
        bounds.append((0.0, ub))

    res = None
    try:
        res = linprog(c, A_ub=A, b_ub=b_ub, bounds=bounds, method="highs", options={"disp": False})
    except Exception:
        try:
            res = linprog(c, A_ub=A, b_ub=b_ub, bounds=bounds, method="revised simplex", options={"disp": False})
        except Exception:
            return None

    if res is None or not bool(getattr(res, "success", False)):
        return None

    g = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    g = np.nan_to_num(g, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    g = np.maximum(g, 0.0)
    s = float(g.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return None
    g = g / s

    best_a = None
    best_score = _score_of(a)
    for t in (0.05, 0.10, 0.20, 0.35, 0.55):
        a2 = _normalize((1.0 - t) * a + t * g)
        sc = _score_of(a2)
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
            best_score = float(sc)
            best_a = a2

    if best_a is None:
        return None
    return best_a, float(best_score)


def _sota_loop(a, deadline, rng, max_iters=50):
    a = _normalize(a)
    n = a.size
    score = _score_of(a)
    best_a = a.copy()
    best_score = float(score)

    if n < 16 or n > 1024 or linprog is None:
        return best_a, best_score

    fails = 0
    for _ in range(max(0, int(max_iters))):
        if time.monotonic() > deadline:
            break

        res = _lp_sota_update(a)
        if res is not None:
            a2, sc = res
            if np.isfinite(sc):
                if sc < best_score:
                    best_a = a2.copy()
                    best_score = float(sc)
                if sc <= score + 1e-9:
                    a = a2.copy()
                    score = float(sc)
                    fails = 0
                    if best_score < 1.5027:
                        break
                else:
                    fails += 1
        else:
            fails += 1

        if fails > 5:
            a = _random_perturb(best_a, rng, 0.25)
            score = _score_of(a)
            if score < best_score:
                best_a = a.copy()
                best_score = float(score)
            fails = 0

    return best_a, best_score


def _select_candidate(cands):
    best_item = None
    best_sm = 1e100
    for item in cands:
        sm = float(item[0])
        if sm < best_sm:
            best_sm = sm
            best_item = item
    return best_item


def _local_refine(a, deadline, rng, max_lp=1, verbose=False):
    a = _normalize(a)
    if a.size < 96:
        a = _resample(a, 256)
    if a.size > 4096:
        a = _resample(a, 4096)
    n = a.size

    b = _convolve_full(a)
    M = float(np.max(b)) if b.size else 0.0
    if M <= 1e-300 or not np.isfinite(M):
        M = 1e-300
    score = float(2.0 * float(n) * M)

    best_a = a.copy()
    best_score = float(score)

    beta_target = 8.0
    eta = 0.45
    topk = 6
    fail = 0
    no_improve = 0
    it = 0
    lp_used = 0

    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        if score < best_score - 1e-12:
            best_a = a.copy()
            best_score = float(score)
            no_improve = 0
        else:
            no_improve += 1
            if no_improve > 5000:
                no_improve = 5000

        K = int(min(2 * n - 1, max(4, topk + no_improve // 100)))
        beta = min(1e6, beta_target / max(M, 1e-12))
        _, smooth_val = _lse_and_p(b, beta)

        L = 2 * n - 1
        if K >= L:
            p = _lse_and_p(b, beta)[0]
        else:
            idx = np.argpartition(b, -K)[-K:]
            vals = b[idx]
            w = beta * (vals - M)
            w = np.maximum(w, -745.0)
            e = np.exp(w)
            se = float(e.sum())
            if se > 1e-300 and np.isfinite(se):
                p = np.zeros(L, dtype=np.float64)
                p[idx] = e / se
            else:
                p = _lse_and_p(b, beta)[0]

        grad = _weighted_grad(a, p, n)
        grad_c = grad - float(np.mean(grad))
        gmax = float(np.max(np.abs(grad_c))) if grad_c.size else 0.0

        if gmax <= 1e-14:
            a = _random_perturb(best_a, rng, 0.35)
            b = _convolve_full(a)
            M = float(np.max(b)) if b.size else 0.0
            if M <= 1e-300:
                M = 1e-300
            score = float(2.0 * float(n) * M)
            fail = 0
            beta_target = max(6.0, beta_target * 0.7)
            eta = 0.45
            topk = max(6, topk // 2)
            it += 1
            if verbose and it % 2000 == 0:
                print(
                    "local n={} it={} score={:.8f} best={:.8f} beta_t={:.1f}".format(
                        n, it, score, best_score, beta_target
                    ),
                    flush=True,
                )
            continue

        scale = max(float(np.mean(a)), 1e-12)
        d = grad_c / scale

        breg_cands = []
        for mult in (eta, 0.5 * eta, 1.5 * eta):
            a2 = a * np.exp(-float(mult) * d)
            a2 = np.nan_to_num(a2, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
            a2 = np.maximum(a2, 0.0)
            ss = float(a2.sum())
            if ss > 1e-300 and np.isfinite(ss):
                a2 = a2 / ss
                b2 = _convolve_full(a2)
                m2 = float(np.max(b2)) if b2.size else 0.0
                sc2 = float(2.0 * float(n) * m2)
                sm2 = _lse_value(b2, beta)
                breg_cands.append((sm2, sc2, a2.copy(), b2, m2))

        accepted = False
        item = _select_candidate(breg_cands)
        if item is not None and item[0] < smooth_val - 1e-12 and item[1] < score + 0.1:
            a = item[2]
            b = item[3]
            M = float(item[4])
            score = float(item[1])
            accepted = True
            fail = 0
            eta = min(1.25, eta * 1.07)
            if score < best_score:
                beta_target = min(90.0, beta_target * 1.05)
                topk = min(256, topk + 1)

        if not accepted:
            d2 = grad_c / gmax
            step = eta * scale
            eu_cands = []
            for factor in (1.0, 0.5, 2.0):
                a2 = _project_simplex(a - float(factor) * step * d2)
                b2 = _convolve_full(a2)
                m2 = float(np.max(b2)) if b2.size else 0.0
                sc2 = float(2.0 * float(n) * m2)
                sm2 = _lse_value(b2, beta)
                eu_cands.append((sm2, sc2, a2, b2, m2))

            item = _select_candidate(eu_cands)
            if item is not None and item[0] < smooth_val - 1e-12 and item[1] < score + 0.1:
                a = item[2]
                b = item[3]
                M = float(item[4])
                score = float(item[1])
                fail = 0
            else:
                fail += 1
                eta *= 0.85
                if fail % 20 == 0:
                    beta_target = max(4.0, beta_target * 0.85)

        if fail > 80:
            a = _random_perturb(best_a, rng, 0.40)
            b = _convolve_full(a)
            M = float(np.max(b)) if b.size else 0.0
            if M <= 1e-300:
                M = 1e-300
            score = float(2.0 * float(n) * M)
            fail = 0
            beta_target = max(6.0, beta_target * 0.6)
            eta = 0.45
            topk = max(6, topk // 2)

        if (
            max_lp > 0
            and lp_used < max_lp
            and n <= 512
            and no_improve > 250
            and (deadline - time.monotonic()) > 35.0
        ):
            res = _lp_sota_update(a)
            if res is not None:
                a2, sc2 = res
                lp_used += 1
                if np.isfinite(sc2):
                    if sc2 < best_score:
                        best_a = a2.copy()
                        best_score = float(sc2)
                    if sc2 <= score + 1e-9:
                        a = a2.copy()
                        score = float(sc2)
                    b = _convolve_full(a)
                    M = float(np.max(b)) if b.size else 0.0
                    if M <= 1e-300:
                        M = 1e-300
                    no_improve = 0
                    fail = 0

        it += 1
        if verbose and (it == 1 or it % 2000 == 0):
            print(
                "local n={} it={} score={:.8f} best={:.8f} beta_t={:.1f} eta={:.3f} fail={}".format(
                    n, it, score, best_score, beta_target, eta, fail
                ),
                flush=True,
            )

    if score < best_score:
        best_a = a.copy()
        best_score = float(score)

    if verbose:
        print("local done n={} best={:.8f}".format(n, best_score), flush=True)

    return best_a, float(best_score)


def _get_given_sequence():
    seq = globals().get("height_sequence_1", None)
    if seq is None:
        return None
    try:
        arr = np.asarray(seq, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    except Exception:
        return None
    arr = np.nan_to_num(arr, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    arr = np.maximum(arr, 0.0)
    if arr.size == 0:
        return None
    s = float(arr.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return None
    return arr / s


def _external_score(seq):
    fn = globals().get("evaluate_sequence", None)
    if fn is None:
        return None
    try:
        vals = []
        for x in seq:
            vals.append(float(x))
        v = fn(vals)
        if v is None:
            return None
        v = float(v)
        return v
    except Exception:
        return None


def _early_return_if_verified(best_a, best_score):
    if not np.isfinite(best_score) or best_score >= 1.5027:
        return None
    ext = _external_score(best_a)
    if ext is not None and np.isfinite(ext) and ext < 1.5027:
        return [float(x) for x in best_a]
    return None


def propose_candidate(seed=0, budget_s=2400, **kwargs):
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    try:
        budget = float(budget_s)
    except Exception:
        budget = 2400.0
    if not np.isfinite(budget) or budget <= 0.0:
        budget = 2400.0
    budget = min(budget, 2400.0)
    deadline = t0 + max(120.0, budget) - 25.0

    rng = np.random.default_rng(int(seed) % (2 ** 31))
    given = _get_given_sequence()

    if given is not None:
        glen = int(given.size)
        if glen < 128:
            target = 256
        elif glen > 2048:
            target = 1024
        else:
            target = glen
        best_a = _resample(given, target)
    else:
        best_a = _make_exponential(256, 1.0)

    best_a = _normalize(best_a)
    best_score = _score_of(best_a)
    print(
        "init len={} score={:.8f}".format(best_a.size, best_score),
        flush=True,
    )

    # First: refine the best known construction.
    if time.monotonic() < deadline - 1000.0:
        t_end = min(deadline - 1000.0, time.monotonic() + 240.0)
        res_a, res_score = _local_refine(
            best_a.copy(),
            t_end,
            rng,
            max_lp=2 if best_a.size <= 512 else 0,
            verbose=True,
        )
        if np.isfinite(res_score) and res_score < best_score:
            best_a = res_a
            best_score = res_score
            print("after initial refine score={:.8f}".format(best_score), flush=True)

    early = _early_return_if_verified(best_a, best_score)
    if early is not None:
        return early

    # SOTA LP loop on a tractable length.
    if time.monotonic() < deadline - 800.0:
        n_sota = int(best_a.size)
        if n_sota < 128 or n_sota > 512:
            n_sota = 256 if best_a.size < 700 else 512
        t_end = min(deadline - 800.0, time.monotonic() + 160.0)
        res_a, res_score = _sota_loop(_resample(best_a, n_sota), t_end, rng, max_iters=40)
        if np.isfinite(res_score) and res_score < best_score:
            best_a = res_a
            best_score = res_score
            print("after SOTA LP score={:.8f}".format(best_score), flush=True)

    early = _early_return_if_verified(best_a, best_score)
    if early is not None:
        return early

    # Low-dimensional global searches.
    if time.monotonic() < deadline - 650.0:
        t_end = min(deadline - 650.0, time.monotonic() + 160.0)
        seq = _lowdim_search(256, t_end, rng, int(seed) + 101)
        if seq is not None and seq.size > 0:
            sc = _score_of(seq)
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
                best_a = seq
                best_score = sc
                print("lowdim 256 score={:.8f}".format(best_score), flush=True)

    if time.monotonic() < deadline - 450.0:
        t_end = min(deadline - 450.0, time.monotonic() + 120.0)
        seq = _lowdim_search(512, t_end, rng, int(seed) + 202)
        if seq is not None and seq.size > 0:
            sc = _score_of(seq)
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
                best_a = seq
                best_score = sc
                print("lowdim 512 score={:.8f}".format(best_score), flush=True)

    early = _early_return_if_verified(best_a, best_score)
    if early is not None:
        return early

    # Full refinement at several lengths.
    target_lengths = (256, 512, 1024, 2048)
    for n in target_lengths:
        rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
        if rem < 90.0:
            break

        if int(best_a.size) == int(n):
            init = best_a.copy()
        else:
            init = _resample(best_a, n)

        if n <= 256:
            sec = 100.0
        elif n <= 512:
            sec = 120.0
        elif n <= 1024:
            sec = 170.0
        else:
            sec = 210.0

        sec = max(25.0, min(sec, rem - 180.0))
        t_end = time.monotonic() + sec
        res_a, res_score = _local_refine(init, t_end, rng, max_lp=1 if n <= 512 else 0, verbose=False)
        if np.isfinite(res_score) and res_score < best_score:
            best_a = res_a
            best_score = res_score
            print("refined n={} score={:.8f}".format(n, best_score), flush=True)

        early = _early_return_if_verified(best_a, best_score)
        if early is not None:
            return early

    # A few stochastic restarts from the best shape.
    for i in range(4):
        rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
        if rem < 100.0:
            break
        init = _random_perturb(best_a, rng, 0.45)
        sec = max(20.0, min(70.0, rem - 120.0))
        t_end = time.monotonic() + sec
        res_a, res_score = _local_refine(init, t_end, rng, max_lp=0, verbose=False)
        if np.isfinite(res_score) and res_score < best_score:
            best_a = res_a
            best_score = res_score
            print("restart {} score={:.8f}".format(i, best_score), flush=True)

    # Final deep polish.
    rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
    if rem > 30.0:
        res_a, res_score = _local_refine(best_a.copy(), deadline - 10.0, rng, max_lp=0, verbose=True)
        if np.isfinite(res_score) and res_score < best_score:
            best_a = res_a
            best_score = res_score
            print("final polish score={:.8f}".format(best_score), flush=True)

    # Sometimes a slightly different discretization/length of the shape is cleaner.
    final_a = best_a.copy()
    final_score = float(best_score)
    for n in (128, 256, 512, 1024, 2048):
        cand = _resample(final_a, n)
        sc = _score_of(cand)
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < final_score:
            final_a = cand
            final_score = sc

    out = np.asarray(final_a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    out = np.nan_to_num(out, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    out = np.maximum(out, 0.0)
    out = _normalize(out)

    ext = _external_score(out)
    if ext is not None and np.isfinite(ext):
        print("external final score={:.8f}".format(float(ext)), flush=True)
    else:
        print("internal final len={} score={:.8f}".format(out.size, _score_of(out)), flush=True)

    return [float(x) for x in out]
```
